| litespec/core/model#render-generated-regions-from-the-model | [Render Generated Regions from the Model](../core/model.md#render-generated-regions-from-the-model) | convention | guidance | Are generated regions current with the model, or is a replacement generation step recorded? |
| litespec/core/model#resolve-every-page-link | [Resolve Every Page Link](../core/model.md#resolve-every-page-link) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `link-unresolved` for a page link naming no file. |
| litespec/core/model#resolve-ties-by-the-later-decision | [Resolve Ties by the Later Decision](../core/model.md#resolve-ties-by-the-later-decision) | rule | guidance | Each tie-breaking decision carries a later approval date than the peer records it settles. |
| litespec/core/model#retire-records-with-replaces | [Retire Records with Replaces](../core/model.md#retire-records-with-replaces) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `invalid-field` for `replaces` outside a decision and `reference-unresolved` for an unknown target. |
| litespec/core/model#state-the-common-fields-on-every-record | [State the Common Fields on Every Record](../core/model.md#state-the-common-fields-on-every-record) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-shape` for a record missing a common field. |
| litespec/core/model#write-every-record-in-its-home | [Write Every Record in Its Home](../core/model.md#write-every-record-in-its-home) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `record-shape` for a record outside its home. |
| litespec/core/model#write-links-on-the-record-that-points | [Write Links on the Record That Points](../core/model.md#write-links-on-the-record-that-points) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports `reference-unresolved` for a link whose target no record carries. |
| litespec/core/principles#avoid-generic-boundary-names | [Avoid Generic Boundary Names](../core/principles.md#avoid-generic-boundary-names) | rule | guidance | Each changed boundary name carries the narrowest accurate business name, with no bare `bus`, `manager`, `helper`, `processor`, or `service`. |
| litespec/core/principles#classify-record-authority | [Classify Record Authority](../core/principles.md#classify-record-authority) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record with a missing or unknown status, or a draft without `decider` and `decideBy` date. |
| litespec/core/principles#keep-one-authored-source | [Keep One Authored Source](../core/principles.md#keep-one-authored-source) | rule | guidance | Each fact on the changed pages traces to one authored record, with every other appearance derived from the model. |
| litespec/core/principles#locate-package-versions-in-the-manifest | [Locate Package Versions in the Manifest](../core/principles.md#locate-package-versions-in-the-manifest) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a project whose stack versions do not resolve from the manifest. |
| litespec/core/principles#locate-record-status-in-frontmatter | [Locate Record Status in Frontmatter](../core/principles.md#locate-record-status-in-frontmatter) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record whose frontmatter is missing a required common field. |
| litespec/core/principles#name-boundary-intent | [Name Boundary Intent](../core/principles.md#name-boundary-intent) | rule | guidance | Each changed boundary name states its business action and owner. |
| litespec/core/principles#prefer-direct-owned-dependencies | [Prefer Direct Owned Dependencies](../core/principles.md#prefer-direct-owned-dependencies) | convention | guidance | Each new abstraction names the boundary that justifies it, or the wrapping convention that permits it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prefer-local-code-until-reuse-is-real | [Prefer Local Code Until Reuse Is Real](../core/principles.md#prefer-local-code-until-reuse-is-real) | convention | guidance | Each new shared unit serves two real consumers under one accurate name, or the sharing convention permits it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prove-enforceable-boundaries-mechanically | [Prove Enforceable Boundaries Mechanically](../core/principles.md#prove-enforceable-boundaries-mechanically) | rule | guidance | Each changed enforceable boundary names a reference, test, or lint rule that proves it. |
| litespec/core/principles#prove-scenarios-with-tests | [Prove Scenarios with Tests](../core/principles.md#prove-scenarios-with-tests) | rule | guidance | Each scenario in the model links a passing test that calls its use case and asserts its result. |
| litespec/core/principles#refuse-approval-behind-a-draft-decision | [Refuse Approval Behind a Draft Decision](../core/principles.md#refuse-approval-behind-a-draft-decision) | rule | guidance | Each decision behind an approved record shows approved status in its own record. |
| litespec/core/principles#require-current-complexity-activation | [Require Current Complexity Activation](../core/principles.md#require-current-complexity-activation) | rule | guidance | Each added package, project, wrapper, process, cache, queue, or pattern cites a current use case, decision, or requirement. |
| litespec/core/principles#retire-public-behavior-deliberately | [Retire Public Behavior Deliberately](../core/principles.md#retire-public-behavior-deliberately) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a retired use case that still names an entry point. |
| litespec/core/principles#separate-authority-from-implementation | [Separate Authority from Implementation](../core/principles.md#separate-authority-from-implementation) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a record that writes its own implementation status. |
| litespec/core/product#answer-every-constraint | [Answer Every Constraint](../core/product.md#answer-every-constraint) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a constraint page missing a required field, Summary, or What It Requires. |
| litespec/core/product#build-intent-before-specification-before-code | [Build Intent Before Specification Before Code](../core/product.md#build-intent-before-specification-before-code) | rule | guidance | The research, decision, requirement, records, code, and tests for the change, landed in that order. |
| litespec/core/product#build-on-a-draft-decision-before-approval | [Build on a Draft Decision Before Approval](../core/product.md#build-on-a-draft-decision-before-approval) | rule | guidance | The approval on every decision behind the released records. |
| litespec/core/product#cite-requirements-from-records | [Cite Requirements from Records](../core/product.md#cite-requirements-from-records) | rule | guidance | The `fulfills` names on each rule, use case, and journey beside the requirements they carry. |
| litespec/core/product#cite-sources-for-chapter-claims | [Cite Sources for Chapter Claims](../core/product.md#cite-sources-for-chapter-claims) | rule | guidance | A source beside each number and claim in the chapter. |
| litespec/core/product#derive-requirement-coverage-from-the-model | [Derive Requirement Coverage from the Model](../core/product.md#derive-requirement-coverage-from-the-model) | rule | guidance | The requirement coverage rendered from the model, with no hand-kept list beside it. |
| litespec/core/product#explain-each-requirement-in-one-line | [Explain Each Requirement in One Line](../core/product.md#explain-each-requirement-in-one-line) | rule | guidance | The one-line `rationale` on each requirement entry. |
| litespec/core/product#group-product-records-by-area | [Group Product Records by Area](../core/product.md#group-product-records-by-area) | convention | guidance | The `docs/product/` layout beside the project's recorded layout. |
| litespec/core/product#identify-every-requirement | [Identify Every Requirement](../core/product.md#identify-every-requirement) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a requirement entry without identifier, `kind`, or `statement`, or a business requirement without dated values. |
| litespec/core/product#keep-behavior-out-of-product-pages | [Keep Behavior Out of Product Pages](../core/product.md#keep-behavior-out-of-product-pages) | rule | guidance | Product pages under `docs/product/` and `docs/research/` free of state, result, code, and code names. |
| litespec/core/product#keep-product-intent-in-its-homes | [Keep Product Intent in Its Homes](../core/product.md#keep-product-intent-in-its-homes) | rule | guidance | Each brief, requirement page, goal, problem, feature, constraint, decision, and journey under `docs/product/`, and each research record under `docs/research/`. |
| litespec/core/product#load-product-intent-before-commercial-work | [Load Product Intent Before Commercial Work](../core/product.md#load-product-intent-before-commercial-work) | rule | guidance | The brief and the affected requirement pages named in the change report for the behavior change. |
| litespec/core/product#name-product-decisions-in-one-numbered-sequence | [Name Product Decisions in One Numbered Sequence](../core/product.md#name-product-decisions-in-one-numbered-sequence) | convention | guidance | Each decision file named `<number>-<name>.md` beside its matching identifier. |
| litespec/core/product#name-the-person-who-approved-a-decision | [Name the Person Who Approved a Decision](../core/product.md#name-the-person-who-approved-a-decision) | rule | guidance | The `decided` date on the decision beside the approval from its named decider. |
| litespec/core/product#order-each-journey-toward-one-goal | [Order Each Journey Toward One Goal](../core/product.md#order-each-journey-toward-one-goal) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a journey page missing its actor, goal, steps, Example, or Between the Steps. |
| litespec/core/product#place-the-story-before-the-commitments | [Place the Story Before the Commitments](../core/product.md#place-the-story-before-the-commitments) | rule | guidance | The chapter sections ahead of the commitment sections on each requirement page. |
| litespec/core/product#record-each-choice-as-a-decision | [Record Each Choice as a Decision](../core/product.md#record-each-choice-as-a-decision) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a decision missing a required field or section; `litespec model check` reports `decision-decides-nothing` when it settles no record. |
| litespec/core/product#record-research-with-read-sources | [Record Research with Read Sources](../core/product.md#record-research-with-read-sources) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a research record without `question`, `sources`, or Answer. |
| litespec/core/product#reject-an-unfulfilled-requirement | [Reject an Unfulfilled Requirement](../core/product.md#reject-an-unfulfilled-requirement) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `requirement-unfulfilled` finding outside an open known gap. |
| litespec/core/product#replace-a-decision-with-a-new-record | [Replace a Decision with a New Record](../core/product.md#replace-a-decision-with-a-new-record) | rule | guidance | The `replaces` name on the new decision and the retired status on the old one, in the same change. |
| litespec/core/product#review-a-requirement-page-by-its-date | [Review a Requirement Page by Its Date](../core/product.md#review-a-requirement-page-by-its-date) | rule | guidance | A future `reviewBy` date in the frontmatter of each requirement page. |
| litespec/core/product#shape-each-requirement-page | [Shape Each Requirement Page](../core/product.md#shape-each-requirement-page) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a requirement page missing a required section; the `reviewBy` date sits in its frontmatter. |
| litespec/core/product#state-each-feature-with-its-state | [State Each Feature with Its State](../core/product.md#state-each-feature-with-its-state) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a feature page missing its state, Offering, refused reason, or planned date. |
| litespec/core/product#state-each-goal-with-its-measure | [State Each Goal with Its Measure](../core/product.md#state-each-goal-with-its-measure) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a goal page missing a required field or its Why It Matters section. |
| litespec/core/product#state-each-problem-with-its-evidence | [State Each Problem with Its Evidence](../core/product.md#state-each-problem-with-its-evidence) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` fails a problem page missing its statement, Situation, or Evidence. |
| litespec/core/product#state-one-product | [State One Product](../core/product.md#state-one-product) | rule | checkable | `litespec model check` reports no `product-not-singleton` finding, and `litespec consumer` validates the brief fields against the record schema. |
| litespec/core/product#state-the-measure-on-a-counted-requirement | [State the Measure on a Counted Requirement](../core/product.md#state-the-measure-on-a-counted-requirement) | rule | guidance | The `measure` and `by` fields on each requirement whose statement names a number. |
| litespec/core/product#trace-every-choice-to-a-decision | [Trace Every Choice to a Decision](../core/product.md#trace-every-choice-to-a-decision) | rule | guidance | The decision link behind each Position and Refusals entry, with its status beside the page status. |
| litespec/core/product#treat-generated-research-as-source-material | [Treat Generated Research as Source Material](../core/product.md#treat-generated-research-as-source-material) | rule | guidance | A research record or an approved decision behind each position built from generated records. |
| litespec/core/release#apply-release-access-controls | [Apply Release Access Controls](../core/release.md#apply-release-access-controls) | rule | guidance | Authentication and authorization guard every restricted release path. |
| litespec/core/release#identify-immutable-release-artifacts | [Identify Immutable Release Artifacts](../core/release.md#identify-immutable-release-artifacts) | rule | guidance | The release record names the source commit and the artifact reference. |
| litespec/core/release#keep-a-first-release-readiness-record | [Keep a First-Release Readiness Record](../core/release.md#keep-a-first-release-readiness-record) | rule | guidance | One readiness record holds dated rehearsals, green gates, and a rehearsal owner. |
| litespec/core/release#keep-generated-contracts-stable | [Keep Generated Contracts Stable](../core/release.md#keep-generated-contracts-stable) | rule | guidance | The contract diff holds no timestamps, machine paths, or unstable order. |
| litespec/core/release#keep-release-records-non-authoritative | [Keep Release Records Non-Authoritative](../core/release.md#keep-release-records-non-authoritative) | rule | guidance | The record links outward to specifications and scenarios instead of restating them. |
| litespec/core/release#keep-unfinished-behavior-in-draft | [Keep Unfinished Behavior in Draft](../core/release.md#keep-unfinished-behavior-in-draft) | rule | guidance | Each use case with unfinished behavior carries draft status in its record. |
| litespec/core/release#list-included-release-behavior | [List Included Release Behavior](../core/release.md#list-included-release-behavior) | rule | guidance | The record lists each included journey, use case, and extension. |
| litespec/core/release#meet-release-readiness-gates | [Meet Release Readiness Gates](../core/release.md#meet-release-readiness-gates) | rule | guidance | Each included use case carries verified status, with passing journey evidence for each included journey. |
| litespec/core/release#provide-release-data-recovery | [Provide Release Data Recovery](../core/release.md#provide-release-data-recovery) | rule | guidance | Schema setup, backup instructions, and a tested restore all exist for the release. |
| litespec/core/release#provide-release-delivery-controls | [Provide Release Delivery Controls](../core/release.md#provide-release-delivery-controls) | rule | guidance | CI, deployment, rollback, limits, and runbooks are current for the release. |
| litespec/core/release#provide-release-operating-visibility | [Provide Release Operating Visibility](../core/release.md#provide-release-operating-visibility) | rule | guidance | Health signals and trace diagnostics ship with the release, with no secrets in source control. |
| litespec/core/release#record-accepted-debt-as-a-known-gap | [Record Accepted Debt as a Known Gap](../core/release.md#record-accepted-debt-as-a-known-gap) | rule | guidance | Each accepted debt resolves to a known gap record with owner and end date. |
| litespec/core/release#record-operating-readiness | [Record Operating Readiness](../core/release.md#record-operating-readiness) | rule | guidance | The record states limits, alerts, runbooks, conditions, and omissions. |
| litespec/core/release#record-release-evidence | [Record Release Evidence](../core/release.md#record-release-evidence) | rule | guidance | The record holds a result for each listed evidence kind. |
| litespec/core/release#record-the-release-artifact | [Record the Release Artifact](../core/release.md#record-the-release-artifact) | rule | guidance | One release record names the immutable artifact. |
| litespec/core/release#regenerate-application-contracts | [Regenerate Application Contracts](../core/release.md#regenerate-application-contracts) | rule | guidance | Committed contracts match a fresh generation from their source. |
| litespec/core/release#report-verification-exactly | [Report Verification Exactly](../core/release.md#report-verification-exactly) | rule | guidance | The completion report lists commands, outcomes, scope, omissions, generated differences, scope limits, and covered use cases. |
| litespec/core/release#resolve-release-blockers | [Resolve Release Blockers](../core/release.md#resolve-release-blockers) | rule | guidance | Each open decision and external claim behind the release shows settled, or the owning records exclude the affected behavior. |
| litespec/core/release#run-backend-release-checks | [Run Backend Release Checks](../core/release.md#run-backend-release-checks) | rule | guidance | The release build and every required backend suite report passing for the change. |
| litespec/core/release#run-boundary-selected-checks | [Run Boundary-Selected Checks](../core/release.md#run-boundary-selected-checks) | rule | guidance | The change report lists every check its changed boundaries select, each with a passing result. |
| litespec/core/release#run-consistency-checks | [Run Consistency Checks](../core/release.md#run-consistency-checks) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` exits zero. |
| litespec/core/release#run-extension-checks | [Run Extension Checks](../core/release.md#run-extension-checks) | rule | guidance | Each affected extension shows its stated verification evidence. |
| litespec/core/release#run-frontend-release-checks | [Run Frontend Release Checks](../core/release.md#run-frontend-release-checks) | rule | guidance | Install, lint, type checks, tests, and the production build all report passing for the change. |
| litespec/core/release#run-journey-checks | [Run Journey Checks](../core/release.md#run-journey-checks) | rule | guidance | The journey tests report passing against the deployed boundary. |
| litespec/core/release#state-each-release-as-a-complete-record | [State Each Release as a Complete Record](../core/release.md#state-each-release-as-a-complete-record) | rule | guidance | The record alone lets a reader judge the release. |
| litespec/core/release#store-release-records-together | [Store Release Records Together](../core/release.md#store-release-records-together) | convention | guidance | Each release record sits under `docs/releases/`, or the release-path convention names its path. |
| litespec/core/release#treat-check-warnings-as-failures | [Treat Check Warnings as Failures](../core/release.md#treat-check-warnings-as-failures) | convention | guidance | The build fails on warnings except for recorded narrow suppressions, or the warnings convention names another policy. |
| litespec/core/release#verify-connected-journeys | [Verify Connected Journeys](../core/release.md#verify-connected-journeys) | rule | guidance | Each completed journey test runs verified use cases to one observable outcome through the deployed public boundary. |
| litespec/core/scope#answer-the-constraints-question | [Answer the Constraints Question](../core/scope.md#answer-the-constraints-question) | rule | guidance | `litespec.project.json` states which outside constraints apply, or records an empty answer. |

| litespec/core/scope#declare-a-consumer-that-has-no-backend | [Declare a Consumer That Has No Backend](../core/scope.md#declare-a-consumer-that-has-no-backend) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` refuses a `client-only` project that names a backend stack. |
| litespec/core/scope#keep-one-bounded-context | [Keep One Bounded Context](../core/scope.md#keep-one-bounded-context) | rule | guidance | Module pages and `litespec.project.json` name one business language and one deployment boundary, with no second of either. |
| litespec/core/scope#measure-capacity-before-expansion | [Measure Capacity Before Expansion](../core/scope.md#measure-capacity-before-expansion) | convention | guidance | Each added cache, replica, queue, or partition follows recorded load, latency target, and observed constraint, or a capacity convention. |
| litespec/core/scope#record-selected-extensions | [Record Selected Extensions](../core/scope.md#record-selected-extensions) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` rejects a selection without a criterion or review date. |
| litespec/core/scope#record-unsupported-scope-decisions | [Record Unsupported Scope Decisions](../core/scope.md#record-unsupported-scope-decisions) | rule | guidance | A project decision names each architecture outside the supported boundary the project adopts. |
| litespec/core/scope#select-a-size-a-shape-and-stacks | [Select a Size, a Shape and Stacks](../core/scope.md#select-a-size-a-shape-and-stacks) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` refuses a shape whose backend stack count is wrong and a platform that names no frontend stack. |
| litespec/core/scope#select-conditional-extensions-explicitly | [Select Conditional Extensions Explicitly](../core/scope.md#select-conditional-extensions-explicitly) | rule | guidance | Each entry in `selectedExtensions` carries a criterion that holds, and no applicable extension from the extension catalog stays unselected. |
| litespec/core/scope#start-with-one-api-and-database | [Start with One API and Database](../core/scope.md#start-with-one-api-and-database) | convention | guidance | The topology holds one API deployable and one database, or a topology convention records what runs instead. |
| litespec/core/stacks#apply-the-complete-stack | [Apply the Complete Stack](../core/stacks.md#apply-the-complete-stack) | rule | guidance | The consumer file set covers every page in its stack folders, the folders above them, and their stackless subfolders. |
| litespec/core/stacks#declare-replacements | [Declare Replacements](../core/stacks.md#declare-replacements) | rule | guidance | Each extension or override names every baseline provision ID it replaces, with the rest staying active. |
| litespec/core/stacks#keep-the-stack-visible | [Keep the Stack Visible](../core/stacks.md#keep-the-stack-visible) | convention | guidance | `litespec.project.json` names each selected stack, and the root `AGENTS.md` names the solution, applications, and commands. |
| litespec/core/stacks#use-manifest-version-pins | [Use Manifest Version Pins](../core/stacks.md#use-manifest-version-pins) | rule | guidance | Each resolved SDK, framework, and package version matches its pin in `litespec.manifest.json`. |
| litespec/core/system#deliver-one-complete-use-case | [Deliver One Complete Use Case](../core/system.md#deliver-one-complete-use-case) | rule | guidance | The use case page with its behavior, persistence, entry points, tests, and operating records present. |
| litespec/core/system#drive-work-from-approved-records | [Drive Work from Approved Records](../core/system.md#drive-work-from-approved-records) | rule | guidance | The change report listing the approved use case, module, product, and journey records the work started from. |
| litespec/core/system#group-module-use-case-files-by-aggregate | [Group Module Use Case Files by Aggregate](../core/system.md#group-module-use-case-files-by-aggregate) | convention | guidance | Each use case file under its module directory or its aggregate subdirectory. |
| litespec/core/system#keep-decision-authority-with-accountable-people | [Keep Decision Authority with Accountable People](../core/system.md#keep-decision-authority-with-accountable-people) | rule | guidance | The draft decision pages under `docs/product/decisions/` naming the open question, the decider, and the decide-by date. |
| litespec/core/system#keep-operational-and-security-references-under-operations | [Keep Operational and Security References Under Operations](../core/system.md#keep-operational-and-security-references-under-operations) | convention | guidance | Operating and security reference prose under `docs/operations/`, or the project documentation naming the replacement home. |
| litespec/core/system#update-records-with-behavior | [Update Records with Behavior](../core/system.md#update-records-with-behavior) | rule | guidance | The behavior change with its use case page, tests, contracts, product records, decision, journey, and operating records changed together. |
| litespec/core/system#use-established-technical-terms | [Use Established Technical Terms](../core/system.md#use-established-technical-terms) | convention | guidance | Prose using aggregate, command, query, reaction, repository, projection, outbox, idempotency, and transaction with their accepted meanings. |
| litespec/core/system#use-ordinary-capitalization-in-prose | [Use Ordinary Capitalization in Prose](../core/system.md#use-ordinary-capitalization-in-prose) | convention | guidance | Prose writing module, aggregate, use case, journey, rule, event, and decision in lowercase outside names and identifiers. |
| litespec/core/system#use-this-consumer-documentation-layout | [Use This Consumer Documentation Layout](../core/system.md#use-this-consumer-documentation-layout) | convention | guidance | The `docs/` tree matching the layout, or the project documentation naming the replacement layout. |

| litespec/ext/containers#declare-writable-paths-and-limits | [Declare Writable Paths and Limits](../ext/containers.md#declare-writable-paths-and-limits) | rule | checkable | `ContainersSecurityTests` match the manifest against declared writable paths and limit records. |

| litespec/ext/containers#test-released-traffic | [Test Released Traffic](../ext/containers.md#test-released-traffic) | rule | checkable | `ContainersTrafficTests` pass after new-version traffic shift. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#persist-occurrence-state | [Persist Occurrence State](../ext/jobs.md#persist-occurrence-state) | rule | guidance | Each occurrence row carries scheduled, processing, completed, or dead-letter state with safe execution details. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#renew-only-owned-leases | [Renew Only Owned Leases](../ext/jobs.md#renew-only-owned-leases) | rule | guidance | Replica logs show no renewal after a failed renewal, and the loser stops its occurrence. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#restrict-completion-by-fencing-value | [Restrict Completion by Fencing Value](../ext/jobs.md#restrict-completion-by-fencing-value) | rule | guidance | Completion writes fail on a stale fencing value in the store. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#separate-schedule-definitions | [Separate Schedule Definitions](../ext/jobs.md#separate-schedule-definitions) | convention | guidance | Schedule definitions sit apart from Command behavior, or the project keeps them beside it. |
| litespec/ext/jobs#state-schedules-as-reaction-triggers | [State Schedules as Reaction Triggers](../ext/jobs.md#state-schedules-as-reaction-triggers) | rule | guidance | Each scheduled reaction page names its schedule or deadline in its trigger, in words. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#store-execution-leases | [Store Execution Leases](../ext/jobs.md#store-execution-leases) | rule | guidance | The lease rows in PostgreSQL carry owner, expiry, and fencing value for each occurrence. |

| litespec/ext/jobs#use-execution-scopes | [Use Execution Scopes](../ext/jobs.md#use-execution-scopes) | convention | guidance | Each occurrence resolves scoped dependencies in one disposed execution scope, or the project names another scope boundary in its documentation. |

| litespec/ext/lifecycle#audit-lifecycle-changes | [Audit Lifecycle Changes](../ext/lifecycle.md#audit-lifecycle-changes) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle operation writes an audit record, or a standalone record where audit is not selected. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#authorize-lifecycle-access-paths | [Authorize Lifecycle Access Paths](../ext/lifecycle.md#authorize-lifecycle-access-paths) | rule | guidance | Administrative and restore queries run through named paths with explicit authorization. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#authorize-lifecycle-changes | [Authorize Lifecycle Changes](../ext/lifecycle.md#authorize-lifecycle-changes) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle operation checks its documented authorization before changing the record. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#bound-purge-and-archive-work | [Bound Purge and Archive Work](../ext/lifecycle.md#bound-purge-and-archive-work) | rule | guidance | Purge and archive work resumes after interruption within declared bounds, with visible progress. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#default-to-hard-delete | [Default to Hard Delete](../ext/lifecycle.md#default-to-hard-delete) | rule | guidance | Deletions with no retention or restore requirement remove the row in the delete transaction. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-post-deletion-references | [Define Post-Deletion References](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-post-deletion-references) | rule | guidance | The specification states post-deletion behavior for references, exports, and unique constraints. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-relational-lifecycle-behavior | [Define Relational Lifecycle Behavior](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-relational-lifecycle-behavior) | rule | guidance | The relational implementation states columns, query filters, partial indexes, migrations, and named filter behavior. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#define-storage-lifecycle-behavior | [Define Storage Lifecycle Behavior](../ext/lifecycle.md#define-storage-lifecycle-behavior) | rule | guidance | The document implementation states document state, filtered queries, indexes, purge transformations, and contract evolution. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#exclude-inactive-records-from-normal-reads | [Exclude Inactive Records from Normal Reads](../ext/lifecycle.md#exclude-inactive-records-from-normal-reads) | rule | guidance | Normal queries read only the active state, with deleted and archived records absent. |

| litespec/ext/lifecycle#model-retained-lifecycle-states | [Model Retained Lifecycle States](../ext/lifecycle.md#model-retained-lifecycle-states) | rule | guidance | Each retained record carries a business reason with a named state transition. |

| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-lifecycle-scope | [Record Lifecycle Scope](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-lifecycle-scope) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle record names classification, retention, deletion trigger, purge owner, hold, export, restore, backup, owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-retention-progress | [Record Retention Progress](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-retention-progress) | rule | guidance | Retention runs persist progress that the next run resumes from. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#record-the-release-of-a-legal-hold | [Record the Release of a Legal Hold](../ext/lifecycle.md#record-the-release-of-a-legal-hold) | rule | guidance | Each legal-hold release records actor, data covered, reason, and resumed retention. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#reject-hidden-lifecycle-flags | [Reject Hidden Lifecycle Flags](../ext/lifecycle.md#reject-hidden-lifecycle-flags) | rule | guidance | Each lifecycle transition runs through explicit behavior rather than a storage-only flag. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#separate-query-shaped-history | [Separate Query-Shaped History](../ext/lifecycle.md#separate-query-shaped-history) | convention | guidance | Query-shaped history lives in a read model, or the project keeps it on the aggregate. |
| litespec/ext/lifecycle#test-provider-lifecycle-paths | [Test Provider Lifecycle Paths](../ext/lifecycle.md#test-provider-lifecycle-paths) | rule | guidance | Tests read through normal and lifecycle query paths on the selected provider. |

| litespec/ext/locale#define-one-locale-route-shape | [Define One Locale Route Shape](../ext/locale.md#define-one-locale-route-shape) | rule | guidance | The application routes every locale through one documented route shape. |
| litespec/ext/locale#follow-the-validation-message-grammar | [Follow the Validation Message Grammar](../ext/locale.md#follow-the-validation-message-grammar) | rule | guidance | Each validation message states field label, fault, and accepted input without technical wording. |
| litespec/ext/locale#format-values-with-active-locale | [Format Values with Active Locale](../ext/locale.md#format-values-with-active-locale) | rule | guidance | Dates, numbers, currency, plurals, lists, and relative time render through active-locale formatters. |
| litespec/ext/locale#handle-unavailable-locale-segments | [Handle Unavailable Locale Segments](../ext/locale.md#handle-unavailable-locale-segments) | rule | guidance | Unsupported or missing locale segments redirect under the documented product policy. |
| litespec/ext/locale#keep-locale-selection-explicit | [Keep Locale Selection Explicit](../ext/locale.md#keep-locale-selection-explicit) | convention | guidance | The URL or a documented session preference carries the locale, or the project keeps it in ambient request state. |
| litespec/ext/locale#keep-stable-error-codes-locale-neutral | [Keep Stable Error Codes Locale-Neutral](../ext/locale.md#keep-stable-error-codes-locale-neutral) | rule | guidance | Each stable API error code stays identical across locales. |
| litespec/ext/locale#localize-public-presentation-content | [Localize Public Presentation Content](../ext/locale.md#localize-public-presentation-content) | rule | guidance | Public metadata, form labels, validation messages, and user-safe errors render in the active locale. |
| litespec/ext/locale#name-messages-by-meaning | [Name Messages by Meaning](../ext/locale.md#name-messages-by-meaning) | rule | guidance | Catalog keys use semantic names citing glossary terms, with no source-language sentences as keys. |
| litespec/ext/locale#preserve-locale-neutral-business-data | [Preserve Locale-Neutral Business Data](../ext/locale.md#preserve-locale-neutral-business-data) | rule | guidance | Domain values and API contracts stay locale-neutral outside explicitly localized exchanges. |
| litespec/ext/locale#record-supported-locale-behavior | [Record Supported Locale Behavior](../ext/locale.md#record-supported-locale-behavior) | rule | guidance | The locale decision lists supported locales, default locale, fallback chain, user selection, browser detection, owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/locale#resolve-every-shown-identifier | [Resolve Every Shown Identifier](../ext/locale.md#resolve-every-shown-identifier) | rule | guidance | Shown codes render glossary labels, with raw codes absent except user-facing reference numbers. |
| litespec/ext/locale#split-catalogs-by-module-when-needed | [Split Catalogs by Module When Needed](../ext/locale.md#split-catalogs-by-module-when-needed) | convention | guidance | Catalogs stay whole per locale until measured size moves them to module splits, or the project splits by feature from the start. |
| litespec/ext/locale#store-user-facing-copy-in-catalogs | [Store User-Facing Copy in Catalogs](../ext/locale.md#store-user-facing-copy-in-catalogs) | rule | guidance | User-facing copy sits in the locale catalogs rather than in source code. |
| litespec/ext/locale#use-bcp-47-identifiers | [Use BCP 47 Identifiers](../ext/locale.md#use-bcp-47-identifiers) | convention | guidance | Locale identifiers follow BCP 47, or the project names another identifier form in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/locale#use-one-catalog-root | [Use One Catalog Root](../ext/locale.md#use-one-catalog-root) | convention | guidance | Catalogs sit under one application-owned root, or the project names another root in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#confirm-every-writing-tool | [Confirm Every Writing Tool](../ext/mcp.md#confirm-every-writing-tool) | rule | guidance | Each writing tool refuses until the person confirms the write. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#exclude-secrets-and-personal-data | [Exclude Secrets and Personal Data](../ext/mcp.md#exclude-secrets-and-personal-data) | rule | guidance | Tool descriptions and logs carry field names with no secret or personal values. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#pass-server-instructions-at-initialize | [Pass Server Instructions at Initialize](../ext/mcp.md#pass-server-instructions-at-initialize) | rule | guidance | The initialize response carries a non-empty `instructions` value stating transport, audience, write surface, and refusal behavior. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#record-the-server-as-an-entry-point | [Record the Server as an Entry Point](../ext/mcp.md#record-the-server-as-an-entry-point) | rule | guidance | The entry point list in `docs/domain/entry-points.md` names an `api` entry titled for the shipped MCP server. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#scope-each-tool-to-named-use-cases | [Scope Each Tool to Named Use Cases](../ext/mcp.md#scope-each-tool-to-named-use-cases) | rule | guidance | Each tool description names the use cases and permissions the tool reaches. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#state-the-distribution-and-update-path | [State the Distribution and Update Path](../ext/mcp.md#state-the-distribution-and-update-path) | rule | guidance | The server documentation states install and update steps. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#state-the-refusing-side-on-each-refusal | [State the Refusing Side on Each Refusal](../ext/mcp.md#state-the-refusing-side-on-each-refusal) | convention | guidance | Refusals carry the `Needs:`, `Needs confirmation:`, and `Refused:` prefixes, or the project names another refusal vocabulary in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/mcp#write-one-tool-description-for-one-decision | [Write One Tool Description for One Decision](../ext/mcp.md#write-one-tool-description-for-one-decision) | rule | guidance | The declared tools directory shows imperative titles, declarative behavior, positive parameter polarity, and stated side effects. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#accept-repeated-delivery | [Accept Repeated Delivery](../ext/outbox.md#accept-repeated-delivery) | rule | guidance | Each consumer keeps correct state when the same message arrives twice. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#audit-outbox-replay | [Audit Outbox Replay](../ext/outbox.md#audit-outbox-replay) | rule | guidance | Each replay records actor, reason, time, and failure without editing the payload. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#avoid-automatic-durable-bus-adoption | [Avoid Automatic Durable-Bus Adoption](../ext/outbox.md#avoid-automatic-durable-bus-adoption) | convention | guidance | Dispatch runs on project-owned Marten records, or the project names a pinned durable bus package as its transport in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#await-target-acceptance | [Await Target Acceptance](../ext/outbox.md#await-target-acceptance) | rule | guidance | Completion records follow target acceptance, with no early completion. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#back-off-unavailable-store-polling | [Back off Unavailable-Store Polling](../ext/outbox.md#back-off-unavailable-store-polling) | rule | guidance | Polling during a store outage backs off with rate-limited logging. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#bound-delivery-retries | [Bound Delivery Retries](../ext/outbox.md#bound-delivery-retries) | rule | guidance | Retries run bounded exponential backoff with jitter. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#classify-message-kinds | [Classify Message Kinds](../ext/outbox.md#classify-message-kinds) | rule | guidance | Each outbox record names integration event, journey-step command, or provider side effect, with integration events citing their Event record. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#classify-permanent-delivery-failure | [Classify Permanent Delivery Failure](../ext/outbox.md#classify-permanent-delivery-failure) | rule | guidance | Permanent failures stop at the configured limit with safe investigation and replay data retained. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#define-claim-ownership | [Define Claim Ownership](../ext/outbox.md#define-claim-ownership) | rule | guidance | Each claim takes owner, expiry, and fencing value in a short transaction. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#deploy-readers-before-writers | [Deploy Readers Before Writers](../ext/outbox.md#deploy-readers-before-writers) | rule | guidance | Releases deploy compatible readers before additive writers. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#dispatch-claimed-records-in-worker | [Dispatch Claimed Records in Worker](../ext/outbox.md#dispatch-claimed-records-in-worker) | rule | guidance | The Worker claims pending records, dispatches bounded batches, and records each outcome. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#dispatch-journey-step-commands-in-a-new-scope | [Dispatch Journey-Step Commands in a New Scope](../ext/outbox.md#dispatch-journey-step-commands-in-a-new-scope) | convention | guidance | Journey-step commands run through `ICommandMediator` in a fresh Worker scope, or the project runs them inline in the dispatcher scope. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#distinguish-dependency-outage | [Distinguish Dependency Outage](../ext/outbox.md#distinguish-dependency-outage) | rule | guidance | Store availability reads as a gauge while claimed-message outcomes read as a counter. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#exclude-manually-repeatable-reactions | [Exclude Manually Repeatable Reactions](../ext/outbox.md#exclude-manually-repeatable-reactions) | rule | guidance | Best-effort reactions that operators can repeat manually carry no outbox records. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#exclude-request-transaction-dispatch | [Exclude Request-Transaction Dispatch](../ext/outbox.md#exclude-request-transaction-dispatch) | rule | guidance | Request paths commit eligibility only, with dispatch living in the Worker. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#gate-dispatch-on-readiness | [Gate Dispatch on Readiness](../ext/outbox.md#gate-dispatch-on-readiness) | rule | guidance | Unready hosts stop dispatch and dead-store polling. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#group-outbox-infrastructure | [Group Outbox Infrastructure](../ext/outbox.md#group-outbox-infrastructure) | convention | guidance | Outbox infrastructure sits under `Infrastructure/Messaging/Outbox/`, or the project names another home in its documentation. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#identify-repeated-delivery | [Identify Repeated Delivery](../ext/outbox.md#identify-repeated-delivery) | rule | guidance | Each consumer checks message ID or business key at its Command or side-effect boundary. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#isolate-the-worker-host | [Isolate the Worker Host](../ext/outbox.md#isolate-the-worker-host) | convention | guidance | The outbox Worker host lives in its own project, or the project hosts dispatch inside its request host. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#keep-provider-mapping-with-integration | [Keep Provider Mapping with Integration](../ext/outbox.md#keep-provider-mapping-with-integration) | convention | guidance | Provider mapping sits in the owning integration, or the project keeps it in the shared dispatch path. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#model-outbox-lifecycle-states | [Model Outbox Lifecycle States](../ext/outbox.md#model-outbox-lifecycle-states) | rule | guidance | Outbox lifecycle states form sealed records over an abstract base with documented transitions. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#plan-undispatched-rollback-records | [Plan Undispatched Rollback Records](../ext/outbox.md#plan-undispatched-rollback-records) | rule | guidance | The rollback Runbook covers records the new writer staged but nobody dispatched. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#preserve-message-identity-during-retry | [Preserve Message Identity During Retry](../ext/outbox.md#preserve-message-identity-during-retry) | rule | guidance | Message ID stays identical across retry and replay. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#protect-active-worker-compatibility | [Protect Active Worker Compatibility](../ext/outbox.md#protect-active-worker-compatibility) | rule | guidance | Every active Worker reads the type and schema version of new messages. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#publish-backlog-indicators | [Publish Backlog Indicators](../ext/outbox.md#publish-backlog-indicators) | rule | guidance | The deployment publishes pending count, failed count, oldest pending age, attempts, dispatch duration, and success rate. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#publish-best-effort-reactions-after-commit | [Publish Best-Effort Reactions After Commit](../ext/outbox.md#publish-best-effort-reactions-after-commit) | rule | guidance | Excluded reactions publish after commit success with no staged outbox record. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#record-durable-delivery-behavior | [Record Durable Delivery Behavior](../ext/outbox.md#record-durable-delivery-behavior) | rule | guidance | Each delivery specification documents guarantee, downstream boundary, duplicate behavior, retry horizon, recovery owner, record owner, and status. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#reject-independent-outbox-commits | [Reject Independent Outbox Commits](../ext/outbox.md#reject-independent-outbox-commits) | rule | guidance | Writer sources stage through the business session with no separate connection or commit. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#release-claim-transactions-before-calls | [Release Claim Transactions Before Calls](../ext/outbox.md#release-claim-transactions-before-calls) | rule | guidance | Claim transactions close before the Worker makes network calls. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#requeue-expired-claims | [Requeue Expired Claims](../ext/outbox.md#requeue-expired-claims) | rule | guidance | Expired claims return to eligible work for a live Worker. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#restrict-completion-to-current-lease-owners | [Restrict Completion to Current Lease Owners](../ext/outbox.md#restrict-completion-to-current-lease-owners) | rule | guidance | Record updates succeed only under the current lease and fencing value. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#restrict-message-retry-to-claimed-records | [Restrict Message Retry to Claimed Records](../ext/outbox.md#restrict-message-retry-to-claimed-records) | rule | guidance | Retry and poison handling touch claimed records only. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#serialize-durable-messages | [Serialize Durable Messages](../ext/outbox.md#serialize-durable-messages) | rule | guidance | Each pending durable message lands serialized in an outbox record. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#set-delivery-alerts | [Set Delivery Alerts](../ext/outbox.md#set-delivery-alerts) | rule | guidance | Alert thresholds sit in Limit records derived from each use-case delivery target. |
| litespec/ext/outbox#stage-messages-with-business-work | [Stage Messages with Business Work](../ext/outbox.md#stage-messages-with-business-work) | rule | guidance | Pending durable messages stage in the same scoped session as their aggregate changes. |

| litespec/ext/outbox#store-required-message-fields | [Store Required Message Fields](../ext/outbox.md#store-required-message-fields) | rule | guidance | Each outbox record carries identity, kind, type, schema version, time, payload, tenant, state, attempt, and next-attempt data. |

| litespec/ext/outbox#version-message-types | [Version Message Types](../ext/outbox.md#version-message-types) | rule | guidance | Each message carries a stable type name with an explicit schema version. |

| litespec/ext/plans#gate-behavior-through-permissions | [Gate Behavior Through Permissions](../ext/plans.md#gate-behavior-through-permissions) | rule | guidance | Each gated use case names a permission in `needs` that only an entitled plan grants. |

| litespec/ext/realtime#authenticate-and-authorize-subscriptions | [Authenticate and Authorize Subscriptions](../ext/realtime.md#authenticate-and-authorize-subscriptions) | rule | guidance | The connection tests show authenticated connections only, with each scoped subscription authorized. |

| litespec/ext/realtime#record-transport-support | [Record Transport Support](../ext/realtime.md#record-transport-support) | rule | guidance | The use case page names its transport choice with the expected hosted-platform support. |

| litespec/ext/report#keep-report-providers-in-infrastructure | [Keep Report Providers in Infrastructure](../ext/report.md#keep-report-providers-in-infrastructure) | convention | guidance | SQL and storage code sit in Infrastructure, or the report-provider convention names where they live. |
| litespec/ext/report#parameterize-report-sql | [Parameterize Report SQL](../ext/report.md#parameterize-report-sql) | rule | guidance | Each report query binds actor, tenant, filter, range, sort, and pagination values as parameters in code. |

| litespec/ext/report#review-report-plans | [Review Report Plans](../ext/report.md#review-report-plans) | rule | guidance | The review record shows query plans and indexes checked against representative data. |

| litespec/ext/report#support-report-cancellation | [Support Report Cancellation](../ext/report.md#support-report-cancellation) | rule | guidance | Report work cancels on request and expired output clears in the cancellation tests. |

| litespec/ext/site#generate-a-sitemap-covering-every-page | [Generate a Sitemap Covering Every Page](../ext/site.md#generate-a-sitemap-covering-every-page) | rule | guidance | The generated sitemap lists every page with its language alternates. |

| litespec/ext/site#mark-unfinished-facts-with-visible-placeholders | [Mark Unfinished Facts with Visible Placeholders](../ext/site.md#mark-unfinished-facts-with-visible-placeholders) | rule | guidance | Each missing fact shows a visible placeholder naming it on its page. |

| litespec/ext/tenancy#record-the-tenant-adoption-decision | [Record the Tenant Adoption Decision](../ext/tenancy.md#record-the-tenant-adoption-decision) | rule | guidance | The tenant adoption decision under `docs/product/decisions/` names source, storage model, isolation guarantee, administrative access, evolution path, and enumeration bound. |

| litespec/ext/tenancy#resolve-tenant-identity-from-trusted-context | [Resolve Tenant Identity from Trusted Context](../ext/tenancy.md#resolve-tenant-identity-from-trusted-context) | rule | guidance | Each trusted host boundary resolves the tenant from verified claims, host mapping, or another authenticated source. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-tenant-operations | [Scope Tenant Operations](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-tenant-operations) | rule | guidance | The backup, restore, replay, deletion, export, support, and investigation procedures each state one-tenant or all-tenant scope. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#scope-tenant-owned-records | [Scope Tenant-Owned Records](../ext/tenancy.md#scope-tenant-owned-records) | rule | guidance | The documents, queries, constraints, cache keys, outbox records, jobs, exports, and storage paths in code each carry tenant scope. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-one-tenant-accessor | [Use One Tenant Accessor](../ext/tenancy.md#use-one-tenant-accessor) | convention | guidance | One current-tenant accessor serves the trusted host boundary, or the tenant-access convention names where readers find the tenant. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-selected-tenant-storage-support | [Use Selected Tenant Storage Support](../ext/tenancy.md#use-selected-tenant-storage-support) | rule | guidance | The session-creation code of each provider resolves the tenant through that provider's tenancy support. |
| litespec/ext/tenancy#use-typed-tenant-identities | [Use Typed Tenant Identities](../ext/tenancy.md#use-typed-tenant-identities) | convention | guidance | Tenant references carry the `TenantId` type, or the tenant-identity convention names another type. |

| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-address-parameters-validated-by-a-schema | [Keep Address Parameters Validated by a Schema](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-address-parameters-validated-by-a-schema) | rule | guidance | Routes declare address parameters, filters, and page in a validated schema. | litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit | [Keep Authenticated Caching Explicit](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-authenticated-caching-explicit) | rule | guidance | Cached actor-specific reads declare cache key, partition, invalidation owner, and security review beside the read definition. | litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-layouts-stable | [Keep Layouts Stable](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-layouts-stable) | convention | guidance | Layouts hold only the frame and providers every child route needs. Look in the layout definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge | [Keep Proxy Behavior at the Edge](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-proxy-behavior-at-the-edge) | rule | guidance | Proxy rules route only, with identity and authorization decided at the API. Look in the proxy rules. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#keep-server-only-code-identifiable | [Keep Server-Only Code Identifiable](../frontend/rendering.md#keep-server-only-code-identifiable) | convention | guidance | Modules reading request headers, cookies, or server secrets sit in files named and placed for the server. Look in the server file names and places. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#load-each-route-and-each-language-separately | [Load Each Route and Each Language Separately](../frontend/rendering.md#load-each-route-and-each-language-separately) | rule | guidance | Routes and languages ship as separate downloads with no icon set or language loaded whole. Look in the route bundles and the dictionary loader. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#load-route-data-before-rendering | [Load Route Data Before Rendering](../frontend/rendering.md#load-route-data-before-rendering) | rule | guidance | Routes load each read through one shared cache before the view renders. Look in the route loaders and the cache definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#prefetch-a-route-on-intent | [Prefetch a Route on Intent](../frontend/rendering.md#prefetch-a-route-on-intent) | rule | guidance | Intent toward a route prefetches into the shared cache only, with no second router cache. Look in the router prefetch wiring. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#read-route-data-from-the-route-cache | [Read Route Data from the Route Cache](../frontend/rendering.md#read-route-data-from-the-route-cache) | rule | guidance | Views read through the cache entry their route filled, and failed reads reach the route error boundary. Look in the view data reads and the route loaders. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#represent-route-states | [Represent Route States](../frontend/rendering.md#represent-route-states) | rule | guidance | Data-driven routes define loading, empty, error, forbidden, not-found, and ready behavior, and an absent address renders the shared outcome page. Look in the route state definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/rendering#use-a-shared-layout-for-a-shell | [Use a Shared Layout for a Shell](../frontend/rendering.md#use-a-shared-layout-for-a-shell) | convention | guidance | Shells sit in shared layouts adding no address segment. Look in the layout definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-a-route-entry-thin | [Keep a Route Entry Thin](../frontend/structure.md#keep-a-route-entry-thin) | rule | guidance | Route entries hold input, load, and component, with reusable logic in modules and no route-to-route reference. Look in the route entries. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-a-use-case-folder-flat | [Keep a Use Case Folder Flat](../frontend/structure.md#keep-a-use-case-folder-flat) | rule | guidance | Use-case folders hold files and no subfolder. Look in the module tree. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-applications-independent | [Keep Applications Independent](../frontend/structure.md#keep-applications-independent) | rule | guidance | Frontends import no source from another frontend. Look in the import statements. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#keep-shared-packages-non-application-specific | [Keep Shared Packages Non Application Specific](../frontend/structure.md#keep-shared-packages-non-application-specific) | rule | guidance | Shared packages hold no page composition, feature state, authentication policy, or application components. Look in the shared package sources. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#locate-cross-route-values-in-the-frame | [Locate Cross-Route Values in the Frame](../frontend/structure.md#locate-cross-route-values-in-the-frame) | rule | guidance | The frame holds client configuration, session read, language choice, permissions, formatting, shell data, and browser uses. Look in the frame. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#mirror-the-backend-use-case-path-in-kebab-case | [Mirror the Backend Use Case Path in Kebab Case](../frontend/structure.md#mirror-the-backend-use-case-path-in-kebab-case) | rule | guidance | Use-case folders sit at `{module}/{aggregate}/{use-case}/` in kebab case and resolve to the backend folder of the same names. Look in the module tree and the structure test proving the mirror. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#order-imports-from-routes-to-shared-packages | [Order Imports from Routes to Shared Packages](../frontend/structure.md#order-imports-from-routes-to-shared-packages) | rule | guidance | Imports point from routes to modules to the frame to shared packages. Look in the import statements. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#organize-a-frontend-into-frame-routes-and-modules | [Organize a Frontend into Frame, Routes, and Modules](../frontend/structure.md#organize-a-frontend-into-frame-routes-and-modules) | rule | guidance | Frontends separate frame, routes, and use-case modules. Look in the frontend folder roots. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#place-a-shared-file-in-the-folder-that-owns-it | [Place a Shared File in the Folder That Owns It](../frontend/structure.md#place-a-shared-file-in-the-folder-that-owns-it) | rule | guidance | Files read by several use cases sit flat in the narrowest folder holding every reader. Look in the aggregate and module folders. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#place-tests-at-their-ownership-boundary | [Place Tests at Their Ownership Boundary](../frontend/structure.md#place-tests-at-their-ownership-boundary) | convention | guidance | Logic tests sit beside their module, and browser suites share one tests root per frontend. Look in the module folders and the tests roots. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#reject-a-folder-name-that-owns-nothing | [Reject a Folder Name That Owns Nothing](../frontend/structure.md#reject-a-folder-name-that-owns-nothing) | rule | guidance | No folder under `src` carries a refused name. Look in the folder tree. |
| litespec/frontend/structure#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-shared-packages | [Use Explicit Public Entry Points for Shared Packages](../frontend/structure.md#use-explicit-public-entry-points-for-shared-packages) | convention | guidance | Applications import only documented public entry points of shared packages. Look in the application imports and the package manifests. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code | [Assert Each Declared Refusal by Its Code](../frontend/testing.md#assert-each-declared-refusal-by-its-code) | rule | guidance | Evidence specifications reach each declared refusal and assert it by its result code. Look in the evidence specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#isolate-browser-tests | [Isolate Browser Tests](../frontend/testing.md#isolate-browser-tests) | rule | guidance | Browser tests create their own data, authentication context, and expected state, runnable alone in any order. Look in the browser specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries | [Keep Mocks at Owned Boundaries](../frontend/testing.md#keep-mocks-at-owned-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Mocks stand only at owned boundaries, with generated contract types, private functions, and stack-named libraries unmocked. Look in the test doubles. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#keep-test-support-narrow | [Keep Test Support Narrow](../frontend/testing.md#keep-test-support-narrow) | convention | guidance | Test support holds render helpers, one shared API helper, and locator helpers only. Look in the test support files. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#match-test-level-to-risk | [Match Test Level to Risk](../frontend/testing.md#match-test-level-to-risk) | rule | guidance | Pure logic takes logic tests, navigation and integration take browser specifications, and every address takes the route contract. Look in the test files beside the modules and under the tests roots. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#prove-a-scenario-by-calling-its-use-case | [Prove a Scenario by Calling Its Use Case](../frontend/testing.md#prove-a-scenario-by-calling-its-use-case) | rule | guidance | Test steps call the use case and assert its result, submitting money-moving use cases. Look in the evidence specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test | [Prove the Module Mirror in a Structure Test](../frontend/testing.md#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test) | rule | guidance | The structure test resolves the module tree to backend use-case folders and names no refused folder. Look in the structure test. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#query-by-accessible-behavior | [Query by Accessible Behavior](../frontend/testing.md#query-by-accessible-behavior) | convention | guidance | Element queries use role, label, name, or visible text before test identifiers. Look in the test locators. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#read-the-deployment-for-its-identifiers | [Read the Deployment for Its Identifiers](../frontend/testing.md#read-the-deployment-for-its-identifiers) | convention | guidance | Record identifiers come from the running deployment, falling back to an absent identifier. Look in the browser specifications. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#run-browser-checks-through-the-command-line | [Run Browser Checks Through the Command Line](../frontend/testing.md#run-browser-checks-through-the-command-line) | rule | guidance | Browser suites run through the command line per route and per file. Look in the suite runner and the CI invocation. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#test-observable-states | [Test Observable States](../frontend/testing.md#test-observable-states) | rule | guidance | Tests assert observable behavior in the states their situation reaches. Look in the test assertions. |
| litespec/frontend/testing#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests | [Use Network Mocks for Component Tests](../frontend/testing.md#use-network-mocks-for-component-tests) | rule | guidance | Component and logic tests needing HTTP answer through contract-generated network mocks with the generated client in the path. Look in the component tests and the mock generation. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#check-every-route | [Check Every Route](../frontend/ui.md#check-every-route) | rule | guidance | The route suite output shows every discovered route passing each check. Look in the suite output and the CI job logs. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan | [Compose Each Route from One Floorplan](../frontend/ui.md#compose-each-route-from-one-floorplan) | rule | guidance | Routes render exactly one declared floorplan, or record a freestyle decision. Look in the route sources and the suite output reading the floorplan name from each frame. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#declare-a-closed-floorplan-set | [Declare a Closed Floorplan Set](../frontend/ui.md#declare-a-closed-floorplan-set) | rule | guidance | The design contract Patterns section lists every floorplan with its when-to-use rule. Look in the design contract. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference | [Derive Each Page Type from a Named Reference](../frontend/ui.md#derive-each-page-type-from-a-named-reference) | rule | guidance | Routes name their page type reference, and freestyle routes name their decision record instead. Look in the route sources. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#govern-behavior-packages | [Govern Behavior Packages](../frontend/ui.md#govern-behavior-packages) | rule | guidance | Behavior packages name their approving decision record and render through baseline components and tokens. Look in the package declarations and the decision records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#hold-the-declared-base-color | [Hold the Declared Base Color](../frontend/ui.md#hold-the-declared-base-color) | rule | guidance | Token definitions carry only declared baseline values, with each departure recorded as a deviation. Look in the token definitions and the deviation records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-a-blocked-command-visible-with-its-reason | [Keep a Blocked Command Visible with Its Reason](../frontend/ui.md#keep-a-blocked-command-visible-with-its-reason) | convention | guidance | Commands blocked only by state or role stay visible and disabled with the reason beside them. Look in the command surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-layout-inside-floorplans | [Keep Layout Inside Floorplans](../frontend/ui.md#keep-layout-inside-floorplans) | rule | guidance | Route files declare no layout outside floorplan and display composites. Look in the route sources. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-separators-at-the-subtle-step | [Keep Separators at the Subtle Step](../frontend/ui.md#keep-separators-at-the-subtle-step) | rule | guidance | Structural separators and input borders sit at the published border step or lighter. Look in the token definitions and the rendered separators. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#keep-tokens-in-one-definition | [Keep Tokens in One Definition](../frontend/ui.md#keep-tokens-in-one-definition) | rule | guidance | Applications use the shared token definition with no declared value and no literal color in shipped definitions. Look in the application and shared token definitions. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package | [Pin the Baseline in the Shared Package](../frontend/ui.md#pin-the-baseline-in-the-shared-package) | rule | guidance | The shared UI package installs the pinned manifest baseline once, and applications install none. Look in the shared package manifest and lock, and the application manifests. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#prove-every-result-a-route-calls | [Prove Every Result a Route Calls](../frontend/ui.md#prove-every-result-a-route-calls) | rule | guidance | Results called from route code are proved by passing tests or named by known-gap records with owner and end date. Look in the route call sites, the test runs, and the known-gap records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance | [Prove UI Behavior and Appearance](../frontend/ui.md#prove-ui-behavior-and-appearance) | rule | guidance | Changes name the evidence the matrix assigns to their class. Look in the change descriptions and the named evidence. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#publish-a-design-contract | [Publish a Design Contract](../frontend/ui.md#publish-a-design-contract) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports `[litespec/frontend/ui#publish-a-design-contract]` for a missing contract, a missing metadata block, a package mismatch, or a missing required section. Run it at the consumer root. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#record-a-freestyle-route | [Record a Freestyle Route](../frontend/ui.md#record-a-freestyle-route) | rule | guidance | Freestyle routes name the decision record justifying their layout. Look in the freestyle routes and the decision records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#record-a-frontend-outside-the-controlled-contract | [Record a Frontend Outside the Controlled Contract](../frontend/ui.md#record-a-frontend-outside-the-controlled-contract) | rule | guidance | Frontends outside the controlled platforms declare an entry point record naming their platform and replacement boundaries. Look in the entry point records. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#restrict-styling-to-semantic-tokens | [Restrict Styling to Semantic Tokens](../frontend/ui.md#restrict-styling-to-semantic-tokens) | rule | guidance | Styles use semantic tokens rather than raw values. Look in the stylesheets and the shared package definition. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-a-command-surface-by-size | [Select a Command Surface by Size](../frontend/ui.md#select-a-command-surface-by-size) | rule | guidance | Commands use the surface the Concepts table assigns to their input. Look in the command surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set | [Select a Tone from the Closed Set](../frontend/ui.md#select-a-tone-from-the-closed-set) | rule | guidance | Applications declare their state-to-tone mapping once as an exhaustive table of glossary terms. Look in the application state mapping. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#select-one-visual-authority | [Select One Visual Authority](../frontend/ui.md#select-one-visual-authority) | rule | checkable | `litespec consumer` reports controlled frontends with no `paths.uiPackage` in `litespec.project.json`, and `litespec ui` reports `[litespec/frontend/ui#select-one-visual-authority]` for a second general-purpose visual dependency. Run either at the consumer root. |

Look in the error surfaces. |
| litespec/frontend/ui#use-the-product-profiles | [Use the Product Profiles](../frontend/ui.md#use-the-product-profiles) | convention | checkable | `litespec ui` reports a controlled frontend with no `ui` block declaring its density profile or a profile outside `public-light`, `application-balanced`, `admin-dense`. Run it at the consumer root. |

| litespec/frontend/web/components#meet-accessibility-requirements | [Meet Accessibility Requirements](../frontend/web/components.md#meet-accessibility-requirements) | rule | checkable | `tests/routes/every-route.spec.ts` tabs through every route and fails on an unreachable control, a missing focus indicator, an undersized target, or a silent status change. |

| litespec/frontend/web/components#protect-rich-content-boundaries | [Protect Rich Content Boundaries](../frontend/web/components.md#protect-rich-content-boundaries) | rule | guidance | Each stored-rich-content decision record names its sanitizer, allowed elements and attributes, link policy, and test cases, and no component passes untrusted content to the sink its stack page names without one. |

| litespec/frontend/web/components#use-no-raw-element-in-page-code | [Use No Raw Element in Page Code](../frontend/web/components.md#use-no-raw-element-in-page-code) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` refuses each raw layout or interactive element in route and module code, naming the composite to use. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner | [Assign State to the Narrowest Owner](../frontend/web/data.md#assign-state-to-the-narrowest-owner) | rule | guidance | Each value lives in the query cache, the address, form state, or the component state its stack page names, whichever is narrowest. |

| litespec/frontend/web/data#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame | [Keep the Client Boundary in the Frame](../frontend/web/data.md#keep-the-client-boundary-in-the-frame) | convention | guidance | Client configuration, problem mapping, and download helpers sit only in the frame root the stack page names. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries | [Localize Copy Through Locale Dictionaries](../frontend/web/data.md#localize-copy-through-locale-dictionaries) | rule | guidance | All copy resolves through the dictionary library its stack page names, with fill, plurals, one chunk per language, and typed keys with a declared fallback. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type | [Map Every Failure to One Problem Type](../frontend/web/data.md#map-every-failure-to-one-problem-type) | rule | guidance | Each rendered error carries one problem shape decided from its code, with the `traceId` in copyable form in its detail. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused | [Place a Refusal Beside What Was Refused](../frontend/web/data.md#place-a-refusal-beside-what-was-refused) | rule | guidance | Each refusal renders beside its refused record or control through the shared alert, in the application's sentence for that code. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it | [Regenerate the Client Rather Than Editing It](../frontend/web/data.md#regenerate-the-client-rather-than-editing-it) | convention | checkable | `pnpm api:types` regenerates the client, and committed output that differs from generated output fails the contract check. |
| litespec/frontend/web/data#update-the-cache-a-command-changed | [Update the Cache a Command Changed](../frontend/web/data.md#update-the-cache-a-command-changed) | rule | guidance | Each successful command invalidates the TanStack Query entries its command changed and no others. |

| litespec/frontend/web/patterns#implement-a-chosen-pattern-once | [Implement a Chosen Pattern Once](../frontend/web/patterns.md#implement-a-chosen-pattern-once) | rule | checkable | `litespec ui` reports each named React or Svelte component path that does not exist. |
| litespec/frontend/web/patterns#keep-a-visual-registry | [Keep a Visual Registry](../frontend/web/patterns.md#keep-a-visual-registry) | convention | guidance | The product registry renders every product-added option at 390 and 1280 pixels, and `litespec patterns preview` draws every LiteSpec option at 390 and 1200. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/components#keep-component-boundaries-typed | [Keep Component Boundaries Typed](../frontend/web/react/components.md#keep-component-boundaries-typed) | rule | checkable | `pnpm type-check` runs under strict mode and fails on an untyped prop or event. |
| litespec/frontend/web/react/components#write-components-as-functions | [Write Components as Functions](../frontend/web/react/components.md#write-components-as-functions) | rule | guidance | Every component in the workspace is a function component using hooks, with no class component. |

| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/components#write-components-in-runes-mode | [Write Components in Runes Mode](../frontend/web/svelte/components.md#write-components-in-runes-mode) | rule | checkable | `svelte-check` fails on a legacy reactive statement or an untyped `$props` call. |

| litespec/frontend/web/svelte/stack#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience | [Select a Rendering Mode by Audience](../frontend/web/svelte/stack.md#select-a-rendering-mode-by-audience) | rule | guidance | Each frontend renders in the mode its audience requires: static files for a console, server rendering for an indexed public page. |

| litespec/frontend/web/testing#match-test-level-to-risk | [Match Test Level to Risk](../frontend/web/testing.md#match-test-level-to-risk) | rule | guidance | Each test uses Vitest beside its module for pure logic and Playwright against a running stack for navigation and integration, with the route contract visiting every address. |
| litespec/frontend/web/testing#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test | [Prove the Module Mirror in a Structure Test](../frontend/web/testing.md#prove-the-module-mirror-in-a-structure-test) | rule | guidance | The Vitest structure test resolves each module folder to a backend use case and names no refused folder. |

| litespec/frontend/web/ui#keep-layout-inside-floorplans | [Keep Layout Inside Floorplans](../frontend/web/ui.md#keep-layout-inside-floorplans) | rule | checkable | `pnpm eslint` refuses each layout utility in route code, naming the floorplan or composite to use. |
